After analyzing the text, I found that the following public cloud services were impacted:

**Microsoft Azure**

* Service: Key Vault
* Region: West Europe
* Availability Zone: Not specified
* Impact Date: 18 Mar 2021 23:00 UTC
* Duration: 2 days, 15 hours, 50 minutes
* Root Cause: Network resource exhaustion due to a surge of requests to the Data Plane for a specific type of resource, caused by a code defect and under-provisioning in West Europe and North Europe regions.
* Downtime Period: Not specified
* Resolved Date: 19 Mar 2021 01:50 UTC

* Downtime Period: Not specified
